# Milestone 01 — Batch 7 Existing Image Inventory

> **Current decision — 27 November 2026:** [Independent review and owner acceptance](https://github.com/Caffoot/Caffoot-Lite/issues/366#issuecomment-7210689297) accept the inventory and bounded assessment, including the eight narrow reuse proposals. The four unresolved candidates remain pending. The dated inventory and class counts below are preserved; [Batch 7A](../how-tos/features-and-instructions/v2.2-draft/screenshots/batch-7a/README.md) records new images separately. No historical file has been moved, edited or deleted.

**27 November 2026. Read-only file inventory and limited visual assessment; independent ChatGPT review and owner acceptance pending.** This accompanies the [Batch 7 reconciliation](Milestone-01-Batch-7-Review.md). No image is newly embedded, altered or promoted.

The inventory covers every raster file in development evidence, draft-guide screenshots and the published v2.1 review gallery: **1,213 files = 1,154 + 49 + 10**. It deliberately includes mobile, superseded and reference-only files so none disappears from the evidence accounting. These are file counts, not requirement groups or accepted screenshots. Other old published screenshot paths remain preserved by the complete tree comparison; they are not additional candidates for current v2.2 reuse.

Classes: **1** suitable for the narrowly proposed reuse, awaiting owner approval; **2** requires recapture for the proposed instructional use; **3** historical evidence only for this checkpoint; **4** outstanding individual assessment or separately deferred. Class 3 does not assert that an uninspected image is genuine application evidence, privacy-safe or current. It preserves its historical record without proposing new use. An older uninspected image could be reassessed later if a specific need justifies it. Class 4 desktop candidates in the latest set need further currentness/privacy/context review before any reuse decision; their absence from the shortlist is not evidence of a defect.

Dimensions below are decoded raster dimensions, which can exceed a full-page capture's viewport. “Desktop-sized” means width at least 1,000 pixels for inventory sorting only; it is not a legibility verdict. Theme, role, viewport, browser, timestamp and source must come from the linked evidence record, never a filename guess. The SHA-256 values are newly computed preservation identifiers, not newly established capture provenance. The native-correction 16-file and Stage 6.6 Batch 2 32-file recorded hashes match (48/48). Earlier Batch 2's separately attributed 68/68 checks remain reused evidence rather than rerun here. The Stage 6.6 Batch 3 report has per-scene revision/browser/viewport evidence but no original per-file hash manifest: its newly computed hashes cannot fill that historical gap.

**28 files opened locally in Batch 7** (eight native tabs and all twenty latest desktop examples); **six earlier Batch 2 visual findings reused**. All other **1,179 files** have no visual certification from this reconciliation. Read the main register for the actual findings, captions, limitations and approval scope. No credentials, QR secrets or personal data are certified absent from uninspected files.

The links enumerate supporting records rather than claiming all their earlier procedures or sources were rerun. The authoritative batch reviews already establish the topic-specific revisions, schemas and historical approval boundaries. A missing record/manifest means provenance is unresolved; no design-source revision is substituted for application source.
